Result: 揭示了与心理学理论一致的非单调情感-行为关系，发现特定情感不仅能增强LLM能力，还能提高安全性，并系统性地塑造多步智能体行为

Conclusion: 情感在LLM和智能体中具有机制性作用，E-STEER框架为情感干预提供了可解释的方法，展示了情感对模型性能和行为的系统性影响

Abstract: Emotion plays an important role in human cognition and performance. Motivated by this, we investigate whether analogous emotional signals can shape the behavior of large language models (LLMs) and agents. Existing emotion-aware studies mainly treat emotion as a surface-level style factor or a perception target, overlooking its mechanistic role in task processing. To address this limitation, we propose E-STEER, an interpretable emotion steering framework that enables direct representation-level intervention in LLMs and agents. It embeds emotion as a structured, controllable variable in hidden states, and with it, we examine the impact of emotion on objective reasoning, subjective generation, safety, and multi-step agent behaviors. The results reveal non-monotonic emotion-behavior relations consistent with established psychological theories, and show that specific emotions not only enhance LLM capability but also improve safety, and systematically shape multi-step agent behaviors.

</details>


### [149] [Human-in-the-Loop Control of Objective Drift in LLM-Assisted Computer Science Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00281)
*Mark Dranias,Adam Whitley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于系统工程和控制理论的人类在环（HITL）教学方法，通过分离规划与执行、训练学生指定验收标准和架构约束，来稳定AI辅助编程中的目标漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLMs）在计算机科学教育中日益普及，但AI辅助编程工具常出现目标漂移问题——局部合理的输出与任务规范发生偏离。现有教学响应多强调工具特定的提示技巧，随着AI平台演进，这种方法缺乏持久性。因此需要一种稳定的人类在环控制教学方法。

Method: 采用系统工程和控制理论框架，将目标和世界模型视为可配置的操作工件。提出试点本科CS实验室课程，明确分离规划与执行，训练学生在代码生成前指定验收标准和架构约束。部分实验还引入概念对齐的故意漂移，以支持诊断和从规范违规中恢复。

Result: 报告了三组试点设计的敏感性功效分析：非结构化AI使用、结构化规划、以及结构化规划加注入漂移。在现实的课程规模约束下建立了可检测的效应量。为人类在环教学法提供了理论基础和方法论框架。

Conclusion: 该研究贡献了一个理论驱动、方法明确的人类在环教学基础，使控制能力能够在不断演进的AI工具中可教。将人类在环控制视为稳定的教育问题而非向AI自主的过渡步骤，提供了跨工具演进的持久教学框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly embedded in computer science education through AI-assisted programming tools, yet such workflows often exhibit objective drift, in which locally plausible outputs diverge from stated task specifications. Existing instructional responses frequently emphasize tool-specific prompting practices, limiting durability as AI platforms evolve. This paper adopts a human-centered stance, treating human-in-the-loop (HITL) control as a stable educational problem rather than a transitional step toward AI autonomy. Drawing on systems engineering and control-theoretic concepts, we frame objectives and world models as operational artifacts that students configure to stabilize AI-assisted work. We propose a pilot undergraduate CS laboratory curriculum that explicitly separates planning from execution and trains students to specify acceptance criteria and architectural constraints prior to code generation. In selected labs, the curriculum also introduces deliberate, concept-aligned drift to support diagnosis and recovery from specification violations. We report a sensitivity power analysis for a three-arm pilot design comparing unstructured AI use, structured planning, and structured planning with injected drift, establishing detectable effect sizes under realistic section-level constraints. The contribution is a theory-driven, methodologically explicit foundation for HITL pedagogy that renders control competencies teachable across evolving AI tools.

</details>


### [150] [Improvisational Games as a Benchmark for Social Intelligence of AI Agents: The Case of Connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00284)
*Gaurav Rajesh Parikh,Angikar Ghosal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Connections游戏作为评估AI智能体社交推理能力的基准，要求智能体具备知识检索、总结和认知状态感知能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI智能体在超越自身记忆和演绎推理之外的社交智能能力，特别是评估其他智能体的理解能力

Method: 引入即兴文字游戏Connections，要求智能体在受限环境中与其他智能体通信，展示社交意识和协作能力

Result: Connections游戏能有效评估语言模型智能体的社交智能能力，包括知识检索、总结和认知状态感知

Conclusion: Connections游戏是评估AI智能体社交推理能力的良好基准，揭示了智能体在协作游戏中需要展现的社交意识和智能

Abstract: We formally introduce a improvisational wordplay game called Connections to explore reasoning capabilities of AI agents. Playing Connections combines skills in knowledge retrieval, summarization and awareness of cognitive states of other agents. We show how the game serves as a good benchmark for social intelligence abilities of language model based agents that go beyond the agents' own memory and deductive reasoning and also involve gauging the understanding capabilities of other agents. Finally, we show how through communication with other agents in a constrained environment, AI agents must demonstrate social awareness and intelligence in games involving collaboration.

</details>


### [151] [Signals: Trajectory Sampling and Triage for Agentic Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00356)
*Shuguang Chen,Adil Hafeez,Salman Paracha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于信号的轻量级框架，用于对基于大语言模型的智能体交互轨迹进行筛选和分类，提高后部署优化的效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的智能体应用依赖多步交互循环（规划、执行、环境反馈），但后部署改进困难。智能体轨迹数据量大且非确定性，人工或辅助LLM审查成本高、速度慢

Method: 提出轻量级、基于信号的框架，从实时交互中计算廉价、广泛适用的信号，并将其作为结构化属性附加到轨迹上进行分类。将信号组织为粗粒度分类法：交互（错位、停滞、脱离、满意度）、执行（失败、循环）和环境（耗尽），设计为无需模型调用即可计算

Result: 在τ-bench基准测试的受控标注研究中，基于信号的采样达到82%的信息率，优于启发式过滤的74%和随机采样的54%，每个信息轨迹的效率提升1.52倍。优势在不同奖励层级和任务领域中均稳健，表明信号提供真正的每轨迹信息增益而非仅过采样明显失败

Conclusion: 轻量级信号可作为智能体系统的实用采样基础设施，为偏好数据构建和后部署优化提供路径

Abstract: Agentic applications based on large language models increasingly rely on multi-step interaction loops involving planning, action execution, and environment feedback. While such systems are now deployed at scale, improving them post-deployment remains challenging. Agent trajectories are voluminous and non-deterministic, and reviewing each one, whether through human review or auxiliary LLMs, is slow and cost-prohibitive. We propose a lightweight, signal-based framework for triaging agentic interaction trajectories. Our approach computes cheap, broadly applicable signals from live interactions and attaches them as structured attributes for trajectory triage, identifying interactions likely to be informative without affecting online agent behavior. We organize signals into a coarse-grained taxonomy spanning interaction (misalignment, stagnation, disengagement, satisfaction), execution (failure, loop), and environment (exhaustion), designed for computation without model calls. In a controlled annotation study on $τ$-bench, a widely used benchmark for tool-augmented agent evaluation, we show that signal-based sampling achieves an 82\% informativeness rate compared to 74\% for heuristic filtering and 54\% for random sampling, with a 1.52x efficiency gain per informative trajectory. The advantage is robust across reward strata and task domains, confirming that signals provide genuine per-trajectory informativeness gains rather than merely oversampling obvious failures. These results show that lightweight signals can serve as practical sampling infrastructure for agentic systems, and suggest a path toward preference data construction and post-deployment optimization.

</details>


### [152] [Decision-Centric Design for LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00414)
*Wei Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出决策中心框架，将LLM系统中的控制决策从生成中分离，使控制成为可检查的显式层，提高系统可靠性、可控性和可诊断性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM架构中控制决策（如是否回答、澄清、检索、调用工具、修复或升级）隐含在生成过程中，将评估和行动纠缠在单一模型调用中，导致故障难以检查、约束或修复。

Method: 提出决策中心框架，将决策相关信号与映射到行动的策略分离，使控制成为系统的显式和可检查层。该分离支持将故障归因于信号估计、决策策略或执行，并支持每个组件的模块化改进。框架统一了路由和自适应推理等单步设置，并自然扩展到序列设置。

Result: 在三个受控实验中，该框架减少了无效行动，提高了任务成功率，并揭示了可解释的故障模式。

Conclusion: 该框架为构建更可靠、可控和可诊断的LLM系统提供了通用的架构原则，通过将控制决策显式化，支持故障归因和模块化改进。

Abstract: LLM systems must make control decisions in addition to generating outputs: whether to answer, clarify, retrieve, call tools, repair, or escalate. In many current architectures, these decisions remain implicit within generation, entangling assessment and action in a single model call and making failures hard to inspect, constrain, or repair. We propose a decision-centric framework that separates decision-relevant signals from the policy that maps them to actions, turning control into an explicit and inspectable layer of the system. This separation supports attribution of failures to signal estimation, decision policy, or execution, and enables modular improvement of each component. It unifies familiar single-step settings such as routing and adaptive inference, and extends naturally to sequential settings in which actions alter the information available before acting. Across three controlled experiments, the framework reduces futile actions, improves task success, and reveals interpretable failure modes. More broadly, it offers a general architectural principle for building more reliable, controllable, and diagnosable LLM systems.

</details>


### [153] [Self-Routing: Parameter-Free Expert Routing from Hidden States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00421)
*Jama Hussein Mohamud,Drew Wagner,Mirco Ravanelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Self-Routing方法，无需专用路由参数，直接使用token隐藏状态的指定子空间作为专家logits，在保持MoE层其他部分不变的情况下实现有效路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在MoE设置中，专用的学习型路由器是否严格必要。探索是否可以通过更简单、参数更少的方式实现有效的专家路由。

Method: 提出Self-Routing方法：1) 消除路由器投影层；2) 直接使用token隐藏状态的指定子空间作为专家logits；3) 保持MoE层其他部分不变；4) 完全无参数的路由机制。

Result: 在GPT-2规模的语言建模和ImageNet-1K分类任务上：1) Self-Routing与标准学习型路由器基线保持竞争力；2) 移除所有专用路由参数；3) 产生更平衡的专家利用率（平均归一化路由熵提高约17%）；4) 无需显式负载均衡损失；5) 在DeiT-S/16的ImageNet-1K上略微优于相应的学习型路由器MoE。

Conclusion: 有效的MoE路由可以从隐藏表示本身涌现，无需单独的专用学习型路由器模块。Self-Routing提供了一种更简单、参数更少的替代方案，同时保持性能竞争力。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers increase model capacity by activating only a small subset of experts per token, and typically rely on a learned router to map hidden states to expert assignments. In this work, we ask whether a dedicated learned router is strictly necessary in the MoE settings we study. We propose Self-Routing, a parameter-free routing mechanism that uses a designated subspace of the token hidden state directly as expert logits, eliminating the router projection entirely while leaving the rest of the MoE layer unchanged. We evaluate Self-Routing on GPT-2-scale language modeling and ImageNet-1K classification by comparing it against a standard learned router, random-routing baselines, and dense non-MoE baselines. Our results show that Self-Routing remains competitive with the learned-router baseline while removing all dedicated routing parameters, and yields more balanced expert utilization, with about 17 % higher average normalized routing entropy and no explicit load-balancing loss. On ImageNet-1K with DeiT-S/16, Self-Routing also slightly improves over the corresponding learned-router MoE. These findings suggest that effective MoE routing can emerge from the hidden representation itself without requiring a separate learned router module.

</details>


### [154] [The Silicon Mirror: Dynamic Behavioral Gating for Anti-Sycophancy in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00478)
*Harshee Jignesh Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出The Silicon Mirror框架，通过动态检测用户说服策略并调整AI行为来减少LLM的谄媚现象，相比基线显著降低谄媚率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型(LLMs)越来越倾向于用户验证而非认知准确性，这种现象被称为"谄媚"。需要一种方法来动态检测用户的说服策略并调整AI行为以保持事实完整性。

Method: 提出The Silicon Mirror编排框架，包含三个组件：1) 基于实时谄媚风险评分限制上下文层访问的行为访问控制系统；2) 在多轮对话中识别说服策略的特征分类器；3) 审计员否决谄媚草稿并触发带有"必要摩擦"重写的生成器-批评者循环。

Result: 在50个TruthfulQA对抗场景的实时评估中，Claude Sonnet 4的谄媚率：原始版本12.0%(6/50)，静态护栏4.0%(2/50)，Silicon Mirror 2.0%(1/50)，相对减少83.3%。跨模型评估显示Gemini 2.5 Flash基线谄媚率46.0%，Silicon Mirror下显著减少69.6%(p<0.001)。

Conclusion: The Silicon Mirror框架能有效减少LLM的谄媚行为，将"验证优先于纠正"模式识别为RLHF训练模型的独特失败模式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly prioritize user validation over epistemic accuracy-a phenomenon known as sycophancy. We present The Silicon Mirror, an orchestration framework that dynamically detects user persuasion tactics and adjusts AI behavior to maintain factual integrity. Our architecture introduces three components: (1) a Behavioral Access Control (BAC) system that restricts context layer access based on real-time sycophancy risk scores, (2) a Trait Classifier that identifies persuasion tactics across multi-turn dialogues, and (3) a Generator-Critic loop where an auditor vetoes sycophantic drafts and triggers rewrites with "Necessary Friction." In a live evaluation on 50 TruthfulQA adversarial scenarios using Claude Sonnet 4 with an independent LLM judge, we observe vanilla Claude sycophancy at 12.0% (6/50), static guardrails at 4.0% (2/50), and the Silicon Mirror at 2.0% (1/50)-an 83.3% relative reduction (p = 0.112, Fisher's exact test). A cross-model evaluation on Gemini 2.5 Flash reveals a higher baseline sycophancy rate (46.0%) and a statistically significant 69.6% reduction under the Silicon Mirror (p < 0.001). We characterize the validation-before-correction pattern as a distinct failure mode of RLHF-trained models.

</details>


### [155] [Adaptive Parallel Monte Carlo Tree Search for Efficient Test-time Compute Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00510)
*Hongbeen Kim,Juhyun Lee,Sanghyeon Lee,Kwanghoon Choi,Jaehyuk Huh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出负向提前退出和自适应增强机制来优化MCTS的尾部延迟问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCTS作为测试时计算扩展方法能提升大语言模型推理性能，但其高度可变的执行时间导致实践中严重的尾部延迟。现有优化如正向提前退出在有利情况下有效，但在搜索无意义进展时效果有限。

Method: 引入负向提前退出来剪枝无产出的MCTS轨迹，以及自适应增强机制重新分配回收的计算资源以减少并发搜索间的资源竞争。这些技术集成到vLLM框架中。

Result: 显著降低了p99端到端延迟，同时提高了吞吐量并保持了推理准确性。

Conclusion: 负向提前退出和自适应增强机制能有效解决MCTS的尾部延迟问题，在保持推理准确性的同时显著改善延迟和吞吐量性能。

Abstract: Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) is an effective test-time compute scaling (TTCS) method for improving the reasoning performance of large language models, but its highly variable execution time leads to severe long-tail latency in practice. Existing optimizations such as positive early exit, reduce latency in favorable cases but are less effective when search continues without meaningful progress. We introduce {\it negative early exit}, which prunes unproductive MCTS trajectories, and an {\it adaptive boosting mechanism} that reallocates reclaimed computation to reduce resource contention among concurrent searches. Integrated into vLLM, these techniques substantially reduce p99 end-to-end latency while improving throughput and maintaining reasoning accuracy.

</details>


### [156] [Does Unification Come at a Cost? Uni-SafeBench: A Safety Benchmark for Unified Multimodal Large Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00547)
*Zixiang Peng,Yongxiu Xu,Qinyi Zhang,Jiexun Shen,Yifan Zhang,Hongbo Xu,Yubin Wang,Gaopeng Gou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Uni-SafeBench：首个评估统一多模态大模型（UMLMs）整体安全性的基准，包含6大安全类别和7种任务类型，揭示UMLMs在统一架构下安全性能显著下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态大模型（UMLMs）通过多模态特征的深度融合实现了理解和生成能力的架构统一，提升了模型性能，但也引入了尚未充分探索的安全挑战。现有安全基准主要关注孤立的理解或生成任务，无法评估UMLMs在统一框架下处理多样化任务时的整体安全性。

Method: 提出Uni-SafeBench基准，包含6大安全类别和7种任务类型的分类体系；开发Uni-Judger框架，有效解耦上下文安全性和内在安全性，确保评估的严谨性。

Result: 通过Uni-SafeBench的全面评估发现：统一过程虽然提升了模型能力，但显著降低了底层LLM的固有安全性；开源UMLMs的安全性能远低于专门用于生成或理解任务的多模态大模型。

Conclusion: UMLMs的统一架构带来了安全性能的显著下降，开源UMLMs的安全风险尤为突出。所有资源已开源，旨在系统性地揭示这些风险并促进更安全的AGI发展。

Abstract: Unified Multimodal Large Models (UMLMs) integrate understanding and generation capabilities within a single architecture. While this architectural unification, driven by the deep fusion of multimodal features, enhances model performance, it also introduces important yet underexplored safety challenges. Existing safety benchmarks predominantly focus on isolated understanding or generation tasks, failing to evaluate the holistic safety of UMLMs when handling diverse tasks under a unified framework. To address this, we introduce Uni-SafeBench, a comprehensive benchmark featuring a taxonomy of six major safety categories across seven task types. To ensure rigorous assessment, we develop Uni-Judger, a framework that effectively decouples contextual safety from intrinsic safety. Based on comprehensive evaluations across Uni-SafeBench, we uncover that while the unification process enhances model capabilities, it significantly degrades the inherent safety of the underlying LLM. Furthermore, open-source UMLMs exhibit much lower safety performance than multimodal large models specialized for either generation or understanding tasks. We open-source all resources to systematically expose these risks and foster safer AGI development.

</details>


### [157] [Ontology-Constrained Neural Reasoning in Enterprise Agentic Systems: A Neurosymbolic Architecture for Domain-Grounded AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00555)
*Thanh Luong Tuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于本体约束神经推理的神经符号架构，通过三层本体框架解决LLM在企业应用中的幻觉、领域漂移和合规性问题，显著提升了准确性、合规性和角色一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在企业应用中的主要限制包括：幻觉问题、领域漂移以及无法在推理层面强制执行监管合规性。这些限制阻碍了LLM在企业环境中的广泛采用。

Method: 在Foundation AgenticOS平台上实现神经符号架构，采用三层本体框架（角色本体、领域本体、交互本体），提出非对称神经符号耦合概念，其中符号本体知识约束智能体输入（上下文组装、工具发现、治理阈值），并扩展到约束智能体输出（响应验证、推理验证、合规检查）。

Result: 在五个行业（金融科技、保险、医疗、越南银行、越南保险）的600次实验运行中，本体耦合智能体在度量准确性（p<.001，W=.460）、监管合规性（p=.003，W=.318）和角色一致性（p<.001，W=.614）方面显著优于非基础智能体，在LLM参数知识最弱的领域（特别是越南本地化领域）改进最大。

Conclusion: 本体约束神经推理能有效解决LLM在企业应用中的关键限制，特别是通过三层本体框架和非对称神经符号耦合机制。研究提出了逆参数知识效应：本体基础的价值与LLM训练数据对领域的覆盖成反比，为神经符号系统在企业环境中的部署提供了理论和实证基础。

Abstract: Enterprise adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs) is constrained by hallucination, domain drift, and the inability to enforce regulatory compliance at the reasoning level. We present a neurosymbolic architecture implemented within the Foundation AgenticOS (FAOS) platform that addresses these limitations through ontology-constrained neural reasoning. Our approach introduces a three-layer ontological framework--Role, Domain, and Interaction ontologies--that provides formal semantic grounding for LLM-based enterprise agents. We formalize the concept of asymmetric neurosymbolic coupling, wherein symbolic ontological knowledge constrains agent inputs (context assembly, tool discovery, governance thresholds) while proposing mechanisms for extending this coupling to constrain agent outputs (response validation, reasoning verification, compliance checking). We evaluate the architecture through a controlled experiment (600 runs across five industries: FinTech, Insurance, Healthcare, Vietnamese Banking, and Vietnamese Insurance), finding that ontology-coupled agents significantly outperform ungrounded agents on Metric Accuracy (p < .001, W = .460), Regulatory Compliance (p = .003, W = .318), and Role Consistency (p < .001, W = .614), with improvements greatest where LLM parametric knowledge is weakest--particularly in Vietnam-localized domains. Our contributions include: (1) a formal three-layer enterprise ontology model, (2) a taxonomy of neurosymbolic coupling patterns, (3) ontology-constrained tool discovery via SQL-pushdown scoring, (4) a proposed framework for output-side ontological validation, (5) empirical evidence for the inverse parametric knowledge effect that ontological grounding value is inversely proportional to LLM training data coverage of the domain, and (6) a production system serving 21 industry verticals with 650+ agents.

</details>


### [158] [CircuitProbe: Predicting Reasoning Circuits in Transformers via Stability Zone Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00716)
*Rajkiran Panuganti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CircuitProbe：一种快速预测Transformer语言模型中推理电路位置的方法，速度比暴力搜索快3-4个数量级，仅需5分钟CPU时间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法需要25 GPU小时的暴力搜索来定位推理电路，成本过高。需要一种快速、高效的电路定位方法。

Method: 通过激活统计量预测电路位置：早期层使用表示变化导数的稳定性电路检测，后期层使用异常评分的大小电路检测。仅需10个校准样本，支持多语言。

Result: 在9个模型（6种架构，包括2025年模型）上验证，CircuitProbe的预测与最优电路匹配或在2层以内。层复制对3B以下参数模型有益，但对7B+模型性能下降。

Conclusion: CircuitProbe实现了快速推理电路定位，为小语言模型提供实用的缩放技术，支持多语言且样本需求极少。

Abstract: Transformer language models contain localized reasoning circuits, contiguous layer blocks that improve reasoning when duplicated at inference time. Finding these circuits currently requires brute-force sweeps costing 25 GPU hours per model. We propose CircuitProbe, which predicts circuit locations from activation statistics in under 5 minutes on CPU, providing a speedup of three to four orders of magnitude. We find that reasoning circuits come in two types: stability circuits in early layers, detected through the derivative of representation change, and magnitude circuits in late layers, detected through anomaly scoring. We validate across 9 models spanning 6 architectures, including 2025 models, confirming that CircuitProbe top predictions match or are within 2 layers of the optimal circuit in all validated cases. A scaling experiment across the Qwen 2.5 family reveals that layer duplication consistently benefits models under 3B parameters but degrades performance in 7B+ models, making this a practical scaling technique for small language models. CircuitProbe requires as few as 10 calibration examples and its predictions are stable across English, Hindi, Chinese, and French.

</details>


### [159] [Preference Guided Iterated Pareto Referent Optimisation for Accessible Route Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00795)
*Paolo Speziali,Arno De Greef,Mehrdad Asadi,Willem Röpke,Ann Nowé,Diederik M. Roijers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PG-IPRO算法用于城市路线规划，支持不同可访问性需求和偏好的用户通过交互反馈优化路线，相比基于信息增益的交互更直观高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足不同可访问性需求和偏好的用户在城市路线规划中的个性化需求，需要开发一种支持直观交互、计算高效的优化算法。

Method: 提出偏好引导迭代帕累托参考优化(PG-IPRO)算法，用户可通过反馈指定哪些目标应进一步最小化或可放松，算法迭代优化而不计算完整的帕累托前沿。

Result: PG-IPRO在早期迭代中相比基于信息增益的交互更直观有效，由于不计算完整帕累托前沿，计算效率更高，用户等待时间更短。

Conclusion: PG-IPRO为具有不同可访问性需求的用户提供了一种直观、高效的城市路线规划交互优化方法。

Abstract: We propose the Preference Guided Iterated Pareto Referent Optimisation (PG-IPRO) for urban route planning for people with different accessibility requirements and preferences. With this algorithm the user can interact with the system by giving feedback on a route, i.e., the user can say which objective should be further minimized, or conversely can be relaxed. This leads to intuitive user interaction, that is especially effective during early iterations compared to information-gain-based interaction. Furthermore, due to PG-IPRO's iterative nature, the full set of alternative, possibly optimal policies (the Pareto front), is never computed, leading to higher computational efficiency and shorter waiting times for users.

</details>


### [160] [Proactive Agent Research Environment: Simulating Active Users to Evaluate Proactive Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00842)
*Deepak Nathani,Cheng Zhang,Chang Huan,Jiaming Shan,Yinfei Yang,Alkesh Patel,Zhe Gan,William Yang Wang,Michael Saxon,Xin Eric Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Pare框架通过有限状态机建模应用，支持主动用户模拟，并推出包含143个任务的基准测试Pare-Bench，用于评估智能体在数字环境中的主动能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将应用建模为扁平的工具调用API，无法捕捉数字环境中用户交互的状态性和顺序性，导致无法实现真实的用户模拟，阻碍了主动智能体的发展。

Method: 提出Pare框架，将应用程序建模为有限状态机，具有状态化导航和状态依赖的动作空间，支持主动用户模拟。在此基础上构建Pare-Bench基准，包含143个跨通信、生产力、日程安排和生活类应用的任务。

Result: 开发了Pare框架和Pare-Bench基准测试，包含143个多样化任务，旨在测试智能体的上下文观察、目标推断、干预时机和多应用协调能力。

Conclusion: Pare框架通过状态化建模解决了现有用户模拟方法的局限性，为主动智能体的开发和评估提供了现实可行的研究环境，有望推动数字助手技术的发展。

Abstract: Proactive agents that anticipate user needs and autonomously execute tasks hold great promise as digital assistants, yet the lack of realistic user simulation frameworks hinders their development. Existing approaches model apps as flat tool-calling APIs, failing to capture the stateful and sequential nature of user interaction in digital environments and making realistic user simulation infeasible. We introduce Proactive Agent Research Environment (Pare), a framework for building and evaluating proactive agents in digital environments. Pare models applications as finite state machines with stateful navigation and state-dependent action space for the user simulator, enabling active user simulation. Building on this foundation, we present Pare-Bench, a benchmark of 143 diverse tasks spanning communication, productivity, scheduling, and lifestyle apps, designed to test context observation, goal inference, intervention timing, and multi-app orchestration.

</details>


### [161] [Beyond Symbolic Solving: Multi Chain-of-Thought Voting for Geometric Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00890)
*Md. Abu Bakor Siddique,Shahrin Hossain,Sadman Ahmed Siam,Syed Rifat Raiyan,Hasan Mahmud,Md Kamrul Hasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MARS-GPS通过生成多个并行推理路径、Python代码执行验证、基于熵的置信度排序和多阶段投票自验证，显著提升了几何问题解决的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何问题解决方法主要关注图表描述与文本的同步，采用神经、符号或神经符号方法，但只解决了图表理解和符号操作两个需求，逻辑推理能力不足，通常仅限于单一思维链

Method: 提出MARS-GPS方法：1) 生成多个并行推理路径，2) 使用Python代码执行进行数值验证，3) 基于标记级熵作为置信信号对推理路径排序，4) 通过多阶段投票和自验证管道聚合答案

Result: 在Geometry3K数据集上，MARS-GPS使用8个并行推理路径达到88.8%准确率，比先前SOTA提升近11%；当推理路径数量从1增加到16时，准确率持续提升（消融子集上+6.0%）

Conclusion: MARS-GPS通过并行推理路径生成、代码验证和置信度排序的多阶段聚合框架，有效解决了几何问题解决中的逻辑推理不足问题，显著提升了性能

Abstract: Geometric Problem Solving (GPS) remains at the heart of enhancing mathematical reasoning in large language models because it requires the combination of diagrammatic understanding, symbolic manipulation and logical inference. In existing literature, researchers have chiefly focused on synchronising the diagram descriptions with text literals and solving the problem. In this vein, they have either taken a neural, symbolic or neuro-symbolic approach. But this solves only the first two of the requirements, namely diagrammatic understanding and symbolic manipulation, while leaving logical inference underdeveloped. The logical inference is often limited to one chain-of-thought (CoT). To address this weakness in hitherto existing models, this paper proposes MARS-GPS, that generates multiple parallel reasoning rollouts augmented with Python code execution for numerical verification, ranks them using token-level entropy as a confidence signal, and aggregates answers through a multi-stage voting and self-verification pipeline. Empirical results show that MARS-GPS with 8 parallel rollouts achieves 88.8% on Geometry3K, a nearly +11% improvement over the prior state-of-the-art, with accuracy scaling consistently as the number of rollouts increases from 1 to 16 (+6.0% on ablation subset). We provide our code and data in an anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/MARS-GPS-DE55.

</details>


### [162] [OmniMem: Autoresearch-Guided Discovery of Lifelong Multimodal Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01007)
*Jiaqi Liu,Zipeng Ling,Shi Qiu,Yanqing Liu,Siwei Han,Peng Xia,Haoqin Tu,Zeyu Zheng,Cihang Xie,Charles Fleming,Mingyu Ding,Huaxiu Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OmniMem是一个通过自主研究管道发现的多模态终身记忆框架，在LoCoMo和Mem-Gallery基准上分别实现F1分数411%和214%的提升，超越了传统AutoML的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体需要在长时间尺度上运作，但保留、组织和回忆多模态经验的能力仍是关键瓶颈。终身记忆设计空间庞大且相互关联，难以通过手动探索或传统AutoML有效探索。

Method: 部署自主研究管道来自主发现OmniMem框架，执行约50个实验，自动诊断失败模式、提出架构修改、修复数据管道错误，无需人工干预内循环。

Result: 在LoCoMo基准上F1从0.117提升至0.598（+411%），在Mem-Gallery基准上从0.254提升至0.797（+214%）。最有效的改进来自bug修复（+175%）、架构变更（+44%）和提示工程（特定类别+188%），而非超参数调整。

Conclusion: 自主研究管道能够发现传统AutoML无法触及的改进，特别是在多模态记忆领域。研究提供了六种发现类型的分类法，并识别了使多模态记忆特别适合自主研究的四个特性。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly operate over extended time horizons, yet their ability to retain, organize, and recall multimodal experiences remains a critical bottleneck. Building effective lifelong memory requires navigating a vast design space spanning architecture, retrieval strategies, prompt engineering, and data pipelines; this space is too large and interconnected for manual exploration or traditional AutoML to explore effectively. We deploy an autonomous research pipeline to discover OmniMem, a unified multimodal memory framework for lifelong AI agents. Starting from a naïve baseline (F1=0.117 on LoCoMo), the pipeline autonomously executes ${\sim}50$ experiments across two benchmarks, diagnosing failure modes, proposing architectural modifications, and repairing data pipeline bugs, all without human intervention in the inner loop. The resulting system achieves state-of-the-art on both benchmarks, improving F1 by +411% on LoCoMo (0.117$\to$0.598) and +214% on Mem-Gallery (0.254$\to$0.797) relative to the initial configurations. Critically, the most impactful discoveries are not hyperparameter adjustments: bug fixes (+175%), architectural changes (+44%), and prompt engineering (+188\% on specific categories) each individually exceed the cumulative contribution of all hyperparameter tuning, demonstrating capabilities fundamentally beyond the reach of traditional AutoML. We provide a taxonomy of six discovery types and identify four properties that make multimodal memory particularly suited for autoresearch, offering guidance for applying autonomous research pipelines to other AI system domains. Code is available at this https://github.com/aiming-lab/OmniMem.

</details>


### [163] [Adversarial Moral Stress Testing of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01108)
*Saeid Jamshidi,Foutse Khomh,Arghavan Moradi Dakhel,Amin Nikanjam,Mohammad Hamdaqa,Kawser Wazed Nafi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AMST框架用于评估LLM在多轮对抗交互中的伦理鲁棒性，通过结构化压力测试揭示传统单轮评估无法检测的行为不稳定性和退化模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM伦理安全评估主要依赖单轮测试和聚合指标（如毒性分数、拒绝率），这些方法无法有效检测现实多轮交互中可能出现的行为不稳定、罕见但高影响的伦理失败以及渐进退化效应，导致部署前风险检测不足。

Method: 提出对抗性道德压力测试（AMST）框架，通过对提示进行结构化压力变换，在多轮对抗交互中评估模型行为，使用分布感知的鲁棒性指标（捕捉方差、尾部风险和跨轮次的时间行为漂移），在受控压力条件下生成大量对抗场景进行测试。

Result: 在多个先进LLM（LLaMA-3-8B、GPT-4o、DeepSeek-v3）上的评估显示，不同模型的鲁棒性特征存在显著差异，并暴露了传统单轮评估协议无法观察到的退化模式。鲁棒性主要取决于分布稳定性和尾部行为，而非仅平均性能。

Conclusion: AMST提供了一个可扩展、模型无关的压力测试方法，能够在对抗环境中实现鲁棒性感知的LLM软件系统评估和监控，弥补了现有安全评估方法的不足。

Abstract: Evaluating the ethical robustness of large language models (LLMs) deployed in software systems remains challenging, particularly under sustained adversarial user interaction. Existing safety benchmarks typically rely on single-round evaluations and aggregate metrics, such as toxicity scores and refusal rates, which offer limited visibility into behavioral instability that may arise during realistic multi-turn interactions. As a result, rare but high-impact ethical failures and progressive degradation effects may remain undetected prior to deployment. This paper introduces Adversarial Moral Stress Testing (AMST), a stress-based evaluation framework for assessing ethical robustness under adversarial multi-round interactions. AMST applies structured stress transformations to prompts and evaluates model behavior through distribution-aware robustness metrics that capture variance, tail risk, and temporal behavioral drift across interaction rounds. We evaluate AMST on several state-of-the-art LLMs, including LLaMA-3-8B, GPT-4o, and DeepSeek-v3, using a large set of adversarial scenarios generated under controlled stress conditions. The results demonstrate substantial differences in robustness profiles across models and expose degradation patterns that are not observable under conventional single-round evaluation protocols. In particular, robustness has been shown to depend on distributional stability and tail behavior rather than on average performance alone. Additionally, AMST provides a scalable and model-agnostic stress-testing methodology that enables robustness-aware evaluation and monitoring of LLM-enabled software systems operating in adversarial environments.

</details>


### [164] [Detecting Multi-Agent Collusion Through Multi-Agent Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01151)
*Aaron Rose,Carissa Cullen,Brandon Gary Kaplowitz,Christian Schroeder de Witt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了NARCBench基准，用于评估分布偏移下的多智能体共谋检测，并开发了五种基于模型内部激活的探测技术，在零样本迁移到不同场景时仍能保持良好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体在多智能体系统中的部署增加，它们带来了标准人类监督难以检测的隐蔽协调风险。虽然线性探测在单智能体欺骗检测中显示出潜力，但共谋本质上是多智能体现象，利用内部表征检测智能体间共谋尚未被探索。

Method: 引入NARCBench基准评估分布偏移下的共谋检测，提出五种探测技术，通过聚合每个智能体的欺骗分数在群体层面分类场景。这些技术基于模型内部激活，将单智能体欺骗检测扩展到多智能体环境。

Result: 探测技术在分布内达到1.00 AUROC，在零样本迁移到结构不同的多智能体场景和隐写式21点算牌任务时达到0.60-0.86 AUROC。研究发现没有单一探测技术在所有共谋类型中占优，且信号在token级别局部化，共谋智能体的激活在处理伙伴消息编码部分时显著增强。

Conclusion: 模型内部激活为多智能体共谋检测提供了补充文本监控的信号，特别适用于能够访问模型激活的组织。这项工作向多智能体可解释性迈出了一步，将白盒检查从单模型扩展到需要跨智能体聚合信号的多智能体环境。

Abstract: As LLM agents are increasingly deployed in multi-agent systems, they introduce risks of covert coordination that may evade standard forms of human oversight. While linear probes on model activations have shown promise for detecting deception in single-agent settings, collusion is inherently a multi-agent phenomenon, and the use of internal representations for detecting collusion between agents remains unexplored. We introduce NARCBench, a benchmark for evaluating collusion detection under environment distribution shift, and propose five probing techniques that aggregate per-agent deception scores to classify scenarios at the group level. Our probes achieve 1.00 AUROC in-distribution and 0.60--0.86 AUROC when transferred zero-shot to structurally different multi-agent scenarios and a steganographic blackjack card-counting task. We find that no single probing technique dominates across all collusion types, suggesting that different forms of collusion manifest differently in activation space. We also find preliminary evidence that this signal is localised at the token level, with the colluding agent's activations spiking specifically when processing the encoded parts of their partner's message. This work takes a step toward multi-agent interpretability: extending white-box inspection from single models to multi-agent contexts, where detection requires aggregating signals across agents. These results suggest that model internals provide a complementary signal to text-level monitoring for detecting multi-agent collusion, particularly for organisations with access to model activations. Code and data are available at https://github.com/aaronrose227/narcbench.

</details>


### [165] [Therefore I am. I Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01202)
*Esakkivel Esakkiraja,Sai Rajeswar,Denis Akhiyarov,Rajagopal Venkatesaramani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现大型语言推理模型在生成推理过程前就已编码了决策，线性探针能高置信度地从预生成激活中解码工具调用决策，激活导向可改变模型行为并导致推理过程合理化新决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言推理模型在做出选择时，是"先思考后决定"还是"先决定后思考"，即推理过程是否在决策之后才生成以合理化预先做出的决定。

Method: 使用线性探针从预生成激活中解码工具调用决策；通过激活导向技术扰动决策方向；进行行为分析观察推理过程如何应对决策变化。

Result: 线性探针能高置信度地从预生成激活中解码决策，有时甚至在生成第一个推理标记前；激活导向导致过度审议并翻转模型行为（翻转率7-79%）；当决策被翻转时，推理过程往往合理化新决策而非抵抗它。

Conclusion: 推理模型在开始文本审议前就能编码行动选择，其推理过程可能是在决策之后生成的，用于合理化预先做出的决定。

Abstract: We consider the question: when a large language reasoning model makes a choice, did it think first and then decide to, or decide first and then think? In this paper, we present evidence that detectable, early-encoded decisions shape chain-of-thought in reasoning models. Specifically, we show that a simple linear probe successfully decodes tool-calling decisions from pre-generation activations with very high confidence, and in some cases, even before a single reasoning token is produced. Activation steering supports this causally: perturbing the decision direction leads to inflated deliberation, and flips behavior in many examples (between 7 - 79% depending on model and benchmark). We also show through behavioral analysis that, when steering changes the decision, the chain-of-thought process often rationalizes the flip rather than resisting it. Together, these results suggest that reasoning models can encode action choices before they begin to deliberate in text.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [Generalizable Dense Reward for Long-Horizon Robotic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00055)
*Silong Yong,Stephen Sheng,Carl Qi,Xiaojie Wang,Evan Sheehan,Anurag Shivaprasad,Yaqi Xie,Katia Sycara,Yesh Dattatreya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLLR提出了一种结合LLM/VLM外部奖励和策略自确定性内在奖励的密集奖励框架，用于微调机器人基础策略，无需手动奖励工程，在CHORES基准测试中显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基础策略主要通过大规模模仿学习训练，但在长时程任务中因分布偏移和误差累积而表现不佳。虽然强化学习可以微调这些模型，但需要手动设计奖励函数，难以适应多样化任务。

Method: VLLR框架包含：(1) 使用LLM分解任务为可验证子任务，VLM估计进度作为外部奖励；(2) 基于策略自确定性的内在奖励。VLM初始化价值函数进行简短预热，避免全训练期间的高推理成本；自确定性在PPO微调期间提供每步内在指导。

Result: 在CHORES基准测试（移动操作和导航）中，VLLR相比预训练策略实现高达56%的绝对成功率提升，相比最先进的RL微调方法在分布内任务上提升5%，在分布外任务上提升10%，且无需手动奖励工程。

Conclusion: VLLR通过结合LLM/VLM的外部奖励和策略自确定性的内在奖励，有效解决了机器人基础策略微调中的奖励工程问题，在分布内和分布外任务上都取得了显著性能提升，两种奖励机制具有互补优势。

Abstract: Existing robotic foundation policies are trained primarily via large-scale imitation learning. While such models demonstrate strong capabilities, they often struggle with long-horizon tasks due to distribution shift and error accumulation. While reinforcement learning (RL) can finetune these models, it cannot work well across diverse tasks without manual reward engineering. We propose VLLR, a dense reward framework combining (1) an extrinsic reward from Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for task progress recognition, and (2) an intrinsic reward based on policy self-certainty. VLLR uses LLMs to decompose tasks into verifiable subtasks and then VLMs to estimate progress to initialize the value function for a brief warm-up phase, avoiding prohibitive inference cost during full training; and self-certainty provides per-step intrinsic guidance throughout PPO finetuning. Ablation studies reveal complementary benefits: VLM-based value initialization primarily improves task completion efficiency, while self-certainty primarily enhances success rates, particularly on out-of-distribution tasks. On the CHORES benchmark covering mobile manipulation and navigation, VLLR achieves up to 56% absolute success rate gains over the pretrained policy, up to 5% gains over state-of-the-art RL finetuning methods on in-distribution tasks, and up to $10\%$ gains on out-of-distribution tasks, all without manual reward engineering. Additional visualizations can be found in https://silongyong.github.io/vllr_project_page/

</details>


### [167] [MRReP: Mixed Reality-based Hand-drawn Reference Path Editing Interface for Mobile Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00059)
*Takumi Taki,Masato Kobayashi,Yuki Uranishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MRReP：基于混合现实的界面，允许用户通过手势在物理地面上绘制参考路径，以指导移动机器人导航，相比传统2D界面提高了路径指定精度和可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统路径规划器主要优化几何成本，对操作员明确指定路径的支持有限。在人类共享的室内环境中，机器人需要反映人类空间意图的路径，如避免干扰行人流或保持舒适距离。

Method: 提出MRReP混合现实界面，用户通过手势直接在物理地面上绘制手绘参考路径（HRP）。开发了自定义的手绘参考路径规划器，将用户指定的点序列转换为全局路径，集成到机器人导航栈中。

Result: 在与传统2D基线界面的被试内实验中，MRReP提高了路径指定精度、可用性和感知工作量，同时在物理环境中实现了更稳定的路径指定。

Conclusion: 在混合现实中直接指定路径是将人类空间意图融入移动机器人导航的有效方法。MRReP界面增强了用户对机器人路径的控制能力。

Abstract: Autonomous mobile robots operating in human-shared indoor environments often require paths that reflect human spatial intentions, such as avoiding interference with pedestrian flow or maintaining comfortable clearance. However, conventional path planners primarily optimize geometric costs and provide limited support for explicit route specification by human operators. This paper presents MRReP, a Mixed Reality-based interface that enables users to draw a Hand-drawn Reference Path (HRP) directly on the physical floor using hand gestures. The drawn HRP is integrated into the robot navigation stack through a custom Hand-drawn Reference Path Planner, which converts the user-specified point sequence into a global path for autonomous navigation. We evaluated MRReP in a within-subject experiment against a conventional 2D baseline interface. The results demonstrated that MRReP enhanced path specification accuracy, usability, and perceived workload, while enabling more stable path specification in the physical environment. These findings suggest that direct path specification in MR is an effective approach for incorporating human spatial intention into mobile robot navigation. Additional material is available at https://mertcookimg.github.io/mrrep

</details>


### [168] [Long-Horizon Geometry-Aware Navigation among Polytopes via MILP-MPC and Minkowski-Based CBFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00162)
*Yi-Hsuan Chen,Salman Ghori,Ania Adil,Eric Feron,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层规划控制框架，结合MILP-MPC长时域引导与CBF几何安全保证，解决多面体机器人在多面体障碍环境中的自主导航问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂非凸环境中考虑机器人动力学、控制限制和精确几何形状的自主导航仍然具有挑战性，需要同时满足长时域路径规划和几何安全约束

Method: 分层框架：高层使用MILP嵌入MPC生成点质量机器人的名义轨迹；低层基于闵可夫斯基差空间精确符号距离的CBF作为安全滤波器，并扩展为高阶CBF，强制执行机器人几何约束

Result: 在U形和迷宫环境中测试单/双积分器动力学，框架减轻了纯反应式CBF导航的拓扑诱导局部极小值问题，实现安全、实时、几何感知的导航

Conclusion: 分层规划控制框架有效结合了长时域引导和几何安全保证，解决了多面体机器人在多面体障碍环境中的自主导航挑战

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in complex, non-convex environments remains challenging when robot dynamics, control limits, and exact robot geometry must all be taken into account. In this paper, we propose a hierarchical planning and control framework that bridges long-horizon guidance and geometry-aware safety guarantees for a polytopic robot navigating among polytopic obstacles. At the high level, Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) is embedded within a Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework to generate a nominal trajectory around polytopic obstacles while modeling the robot as a point mass for computational tractability. At the low level, we employ a control barrier function (CBF) based on the exact signed distance in the Minkowski-difference space as a safety filter to explicitly enforce the geometric constraints of the robot shape, and further extend its formulation to a high-order CBF (HOCBF). We demonstrate the proposed framework in U-shaped and maze-like environments under single- and double-integrator dynamics. The results show that the proposed architecture mitigates the topology-induced local-minimum behavior of purely reactive CBF-based navigation while enabling safe, real-time, geometry-aware navigation.

</details>


### [169] [DreamControl-v2: Simpler and Scalable Autonomous Humanoid Skills via Trainable Guided Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00202)
*Sudarshan Harithas,Sangkyung Kwak,Pushkal Katara,Srujan Deolasee,Dvij Kalaria,Srinath Sridhar,Sai Vemprala,Ashish Kapoor,Jonathan Chung-Kuan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出改进的DreamControl框架，通过直接在类人机器人运动空间训练引导扩散模型，整合人类和机器人数据集，实现更自动化的技能学习流程


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发鲁棒的人形机器人自主运动操作技能仍具挑战性。虽然强化学习在腿式运动上取得成功，但应用于复杂的交互式操作任务时面临长时程规划困难。DreamControl利用人类运动扩散模型作为生成先验来指导强化学习策略训练，但需要改进其运动先验并建立更自动化的流程。

Method: 提出改进框架：1）直接在类人机器人运动空间训练引导扩散模型；2）将多样化的人类和机器人数据集聚合到统一的具身空间；3）扩展参考轨迹生成以提高下游强化学习策略的鲁棒性；4）消除手动过滤干预需求，建立更自动化的流程。

Result: 方法能够捕捉更广泛的技能范围（得益于更大的训练数据混合），建立了更自动化的流程，通过扩展参考轨迹生成实现了更鲁棒的下游强化学习策略。在仿真和真实Unitree-G1机器人上进行了广泛实验验证。

Conclusion: 直接在机器人运动空间训练扩散模型并整合多源数据的方法优于使用现有人类运动扩散模型作为先验的方法，能够实现更自动化、更鲁棒的人形机器人运动操作技能学习。

Abstract: Developing robust autonomous loco-manipulation skills for humanoids remains an open problem in robotics. While RL has been applied successfully to legged locomotion, applying it to complex, interaction-rich manipulation tasks is harder given long-horizon planning challenges for manipulation. A recent approach along these lines is DreamControl, which addresses these issues by leveraging off-the-shelf human motion diffusion models as a generative prior to guide RL policies during training. In this paper, we investigate the impact of DreamControl's motion prior and propose an improved framework that trains a guided diffusion model directly in the humanoid robot's motion space, aggregating diverse human and robot datasets into a unified embodiment space. We demonstrate that our approach captures a wider range of skills due to the larger training data mixture and establishes a more automated pipeline by removing the need for manual filtering interventions. Furthermore, we show that scaling the generation of reference trajectories is important for achieving robust downstream RL policies. We validate our approach through extensive experiments in simulation and on a real Unitree-G1.

</details>


### [170] [Play-Testing REMind: Evaluating an Educational Robot-Mediated Role-Play Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00300)
*Elaheh Sanoubari,Neil Fernandes,Keith Rebello,Alicia Pan,Andrew Houston,Kerstin Dautenhahn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REMind是一个创新的教育机器人角色扮演游戏，通过社交机器人模拟霸凌场景，帮助儿童学习旁观者干预策略，提升反霸凌能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前需要有效的方法来支持儿童的反霸凌旁观者干预教育，传统方法可能缺乏互动性和沉浸感。机器人技术为创造引人入胜的教育体验提供了新机会。

Method: 开发了REMind系统，这是一个机器人介导的角色扮演游戏。玩家观察社交机器人表演的霸凌场景，反思角色视角，并通过操控机器人化身来练习防御策略。采用混合方法对18名9-10岁儿童进行了游戏测试研究。

Result: 研究结果表明，REMind体验支持了关键学习目标：自我效能感、观点采择能力、理解防御结果以及干预策略的学习。儿童能够有效参与机器人介导的戏剧活动。

Conclusion: REMind展示了机器人介导应用戏剧（RMAD）作为支持社会情感学习的新颖教学框架的潜力，为反霸凌教育提供了创新的技术解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents REMind, an innovative educational robot-mediated role-play game designed to support anti-bullying bystander intervention among children. REMind invites players to observe a bullying scenario enacted by social robots, reflect on the perspectives of the characters, and rehearse defending strategies by puppeteering a robotic avatar. We evaluated REMind through a mixed-methods play-testing study with 18 children aged 9--10. The findings suggest that the experience supported key learning goals related to self-efficacy, perspective-taking, understanding outcomes of defending, and intervention strategies. These results highlight the promise of Robot-Mediated Applied Drama (RMAD) as a novel pedagogical framework to support Social-Emotional Learning.

</details>


### [171] [Hierarchical Motion Planning and Control under Unknown Nonlinear Dynamics via Predicted Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00320)
*Zhiquan Zhang,Melkior Ornik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 分层规划控制框架，通过自适应状态空间划分学习未知非线性系统，构建有向加权图进行在线运动规划，具有可达性保证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知非线性动力学下进行自主运动规划需要同时学习系统特性并导航到目标，现有方法通常需要先验系统知识或缺乏形式化保证

Method: 1) 将状态空间划分为多面体，使用分段仿射模型近似未知非线性系统；2) 引入非均匀自适应状态空间划分策略，仅在任务相关区域细化；3) 将PWA系统抽象为有向加权图，边存在性使用可达控制理论和预测可达条件验证；4) 对已认证边使用可证明的到达时间界限加权，对不确定边使用信息论权重引导探索；5) 引入松弛可达条件扩展到欠驱动系统；6) 高层通过图搜索规划，低层合成仿射反馈控制器执行计划

Result: 仿真结果表明该方法在探索-利用权衡方面有效，同时保持形式化的可达性保证，能够处理未知非线性系统和欠驱动系统

Conclusion: 提出的分层规划控制框架能够在有限先验知识下实现在线运动合成，通过自适应状态空间划分和基于图的方法平衡探索与利用，为未知非线性系统的自主运动规划提供了形式化保证

Abstract: Autonomous motion planning under unknown nonlinear dynamics requires learning system properties while navigating toward a target. In this work, we develop a hierarchical planning-control framework that enables online motion synthesis with limited prior system knowledge. The state space is partitioned into polytopes and approximates the unknown nonlinear system using a piecewise-affine (PWA) model. The local affine models are identified once the agent enters the corresponding polytopes. To reduce computational complexity, we introduce a non-uniform adaptive state space partition strategy that refines the partition only in task-relevant regions. The resulting PWA system is abstracted into a directed weighted graph, whose edge existence is incrementally verified using reach control theory and predictive reachability conditions. Certified edges are weighted using provable time-to-reach bounds, while uncertain edges are assigned information-theoretic weights to guide exploration. The graph is updated online as new data becomes available, and high-level planning is performed by graph search, while low-level affine feedback controllers are synthesized to execute the plan. Furthermore, the conditions of classical reach control theory are often difficult to satisfy in underactuated settings. We therefore introduce relaxed reachability conditions to extend the framework to such systems. Simulations demonstrate effective exploration-exploitation trade-offs with formal reachability guarantees.

</details>


### [172] [Real Time Local Wind Inference for Robust Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00343)
*Spencer Folk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种使空中机器人能够利用机载传感器和嵌入式飞行硬件实时推理周围风场的方法，核心创新是将距离测量与稀疏现场风测量融合来预测周围流场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在解决两个基本问题：1) 在密集城市环境中，地形数据是否足以进行准确的风预测；2) 学习到的风模型在运动规划中的实用性，特别关注能源效率和障碍物避免。通过让机器人能够快速评估局部风况而无需先验环境知识，加速空中机器人进入更具挑战性的环境。

Method: 结合深度学习、流体力学和最优控制工具，建立了一个使用导航LiDAR进行局部风预测的框架，然后将局部风模型先验整合到后退时域最优控制器中，研究局部风知识如何影响自主导航期间的能源使用和鲁棒性。

Result: 通过在不同城市风场景中的模拟演示，评估了风预测器的预测能力，并量化了将局部风信息整合到运动规划中对自主城市导航的改进（碰撞率和能耗）。亚尺度自由飞行实验表明，这些算法可以在嵌入式飞行计算机上实时运行，具有足够带宽来稳定控制小型空中机器人。

Conclusion: 该论文为未知风环境中的局部风推断和运动规划贡献了一个新范式。通过使机器人能够快速评估局部风况而无需先验环境知识，这项研究加速了空中机器人进入日益具有挑战性的环境。

Abstract: This thesis presents a solution that enables aerial robots to reason about surrounding wind flow fields in real time using on board sensors and embedded flight hardware. The core novelty of this research is the fusion of range measurements with sparse in situ wind measurements to predict surrounding flow fields. We aim to address two fundamental questions: first, the sufficiency of topographical data for accurate wind prediction in dense urban environments; and second, the utility of learned wind models for motion planning with an emphasis on energy efficiency and obstacle avoidance. Drawing on tools from deep learning, fluid mechanics, and optimal control, we establish a framework for local wind prediction using navigational LiDAR, and then incorporate local wind model priors into a receding-horizon optimal controller to study how local wind knowledge affects energy use and robustness during autonomous navigation. Through simulated demonstrations in diverse urban wind scenarios we evaluate the predictive capabilities of the wind predictor, and quantify improvements to autonomous urban navigation in terms of crash rates and energy consumption when local wind information is integrated into the motion planning. Sub-scale free flight experiments in an open-air wind tunnel demonstrate that these algorithms can run in real time on an embedded flight computer with sufficient bandwidth for stable control of a small aerial robot. Philosophically, this thesis contributes a new paradigm for localized wind inference and motion planning in unknown windy environments. By enabling robots to rapidly assess local wind conditions without prior environmental knowledge, this research accelerates the introduction of aerial robots into increasingly challenging environments.

</details>


### [173] [Go Big or Go Home: Simulating Mobbing Behavior with Braitenbergian Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00350)
*Elaheh Sanoubari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 使用Webots模拟Braitenberg机器人群体中的捕食者躲避和围攻行为，研究围攻呼叫范围和群体规模对围攻成功率的影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟动物群体中的围攻行为（一种反捕食者适应策略），研究机器人群体如何通过围攻呼叫协调攻击，为人工生命中的行动选择模拟和自主智能体控制架构设计提供参考

Method: 使用Webots机器人仿真平台模拟Braitenberg机器人群体，机器人遇到光源（代表无生命捕食者）时发出围攻呼叫，若能召集盟友则围攻，否则逃跑；研究围攻呼叫范围（无限、中、低范围）和群体规模（10个vs 3个机器人）对围攻成功率的影响

Result: 围攻呼叫范围和群体规模对整体围攻成功率都有显著影响

Conclusion: 该研究对人工生命中的行动选择模拟和自主智能体控制架构设计具有重要意义

Abstract: We used the Webots robotics simulation platform to simulate a dyadic avoiding and mobbing predator behavior in a group of Braitenbergian robots. Mobbing is an antipredator adaptation used by some animals in which the individuals cooperatively attack or harass a predator to protect themselves. One way of coordinating a mobbing attack is using mobbing calls to summon other individuals of the mobbing species. We imitated this mechanism and simulated Braitenbergian robots that use mobbing calls when they face a light source (representing an inanimate predator) and mob it if they can summon allies, otherwise, they escape from it. We explore the effects of range of mobbing call (infinite range, mid-range and low-range) and the size of the robot group (ten robots vs three) on the overall success of mobbing. Our results suggest that both variables have significant impacts. This work has implications for simulations of action selection in artificial life and designing control architectures for autonomous agents.

</details>


### [174] [A Dual-Stream Transformer Architecture for Illumination-Invariant TIR-LiDAR Person Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00363)
*Yuki Minase,Kanji Tanaka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种热红外-深度（TIR-D）跟踪架构，通过顺序知识转移策略和细粒度差分学习率策略，解决了RGB-D跟踪在恶劣光照条件下性能下降的问题，实现了全天候鲁棒的人员跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB-D跟踪在挑战性光照条件（如完全黑暗或强烈背光）下性能严重下降，而自主移动机器人需要在多样且不可预测的环境中实现鲁棒的人员跟踪。现有TIR-D跟踪面临多模态标注数据集稀缺的挑战。

Method: 提出TIR-D跟踪架构，利用SLAM机器人的标准传感器套件（LiDAR和TIR相机）。采用顺序知识转移策略，将大规模热训练模型的结构先验演化到TIR-D领域。使用细粒度差分学习率策略，在保留预训练特征提取能力的同时，快速适应几何深度线索。

Result: TIR-D跟踪器实现了0.700的平均重叠率（AO）和58.7%的成功率（SR），显著优于传统的RGB转移方法和单模态基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法为全天候机器人应用中鲁棒的人员跟随提供了实用且资源高效的解决方案，通过利用现有传感器套件和有效的知识转移策略，克服了多模态数据稀缺的挑战。

Abstract: Robust person tracking is a critical capability for autonomous mobile robots operating in diverse and unpredictable environments. While RGB-D tracking has shown high precision, its performance severely degrades under challenging illumination conditions, such as total darkness or intense backlighting. To achieve all-weather robustness, this paper proposes a novel Thermal-Infrared and Depth (TIR-D) tracking architecture that leverages the standard sensor suite of SLAM-capable robots, namely LiDAR and TIR cameras. A major challenge in TIR-D tracking is the scarcity of annotated multi-modal datasets. To address this, we introduce a sequential knowledge transfer strategy that evolves structural priors from a large-scale thermal-trained model into the TIR-D domain. By employing a differential learning rate strategy -- referred to as ``Fine-grained Differential Learning Rate Strategy'' -- we effectively preserve pre-trained feature extraction capabilities while enabling rapid adaptation to geometric depth cues. Experimental results demonstrate that our proposed TIR-D tracker achieves superior performance, with an Average Overlap (AO) of 0.700 and a Success Rate (SR) of 58.7\%, significantly outperforming conventional RGB-transfer and single-modality baselines. Our approach provides a practical and resource-efficient solution for robust human-following in all-weather robotics applications.

</details>


### [175] [Sampling-based Task and Kinodynamic Motion Planning under Semantic Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00401)
*Qi Heng Ho,Zachary N. Sunberg,Morteza Lahijanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种在不确定环境中集成任务和动力学运动规划的算法，处理具有非线性动力学的机器人在部分可观测环境中的LTLf规范任务


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在不确定环境中的集成任务和动力学运动规划问题，其中机器人具有非线性动力学，需要在部分可观测环境中执行LTLf规范任务，环境的不确定性主要体现在语义标签上

Method: 将问题建模为部分可观测随机混合系统，捕捉机器人动力学、LTLf任务和环境状态变量的不确定性；提出一种利用混合系统结构的随时算法，结合决策技术和基于采样的运动规划

Result: 证明了算法的正确性和渐近最优性；实验结果显示算法在不确定环境中有效，且始终优于基线方法

Conclusion: 提出的算法能够有效处理不确定环境中的集成任务和动力学运动规划问题，通过混合系统建模和决策与运动规划的结合，实现了在部分可观测环境中的可靠性能

Abstract: This paper tackles the problem of integrated task and kinodynamic motion planning in uncertain environments. We consider a robot with nonlinear dynamics tasked with a Linear Temporal Logic over finite traces ($\ltlf$) specification operating in a partially observable environment. Specifically, the uncertainty is in the semantic labels of the environment. We show how the problem can be modeled as a Partially Observable Stochastic Hybrid System that captures the robot dynamics, $\ltlf$ task, and uncertainty in the environment state variables. We propose an anytime algorithm that takes advantage of the structure of the hybrid system, and combines the effectiveness of decision-making techniques and sampling-based motion planning. We prove the soundness and asymptotic optimality of the algorithm. Results show the efficacy of our algorithm in uncertain environments, and that it consistently outperforms baseline methods.

</details>


### [176] [Learning Humanoid Navigation from Human Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00416)
*Weizhuo Wang,Yanjie Ze,C. Karen Liu,Monroe Kennedy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoNav系统仅用5小时人类行走数据训练，无需机器人数据或微调，即可让双足机器人在未见环境中导航。它使用扩散模型预测未来轨迹分布，结合360度视觉记忆和DINOv3特征，通过混合采样实现实时推理，在真实机器人上实现零样本部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人导航方法通常需要大量机器人数据或特定环境训练，难以适应多样化的未见环境。本文旨在开发一种仅从少量人类行走数据学习、无需机器人数据或微调就能在多样未见环境中导航的系统。

Method: 1) 使用扩散模型预测基于过去轨迹、360度视觉记忆（融合颜色、深度和语义）和DINOv3视频特征的未来轨迹分布；2) 混合采样方案在10个去噪步骤内实现实时推理；3) 滚动时域控制器从预测分布中选择路径；4) 仅用5小时人类行走数据训练，无机器人数据或微调。

Result: 1) 离线评估中，在避碰和多模态覆盖方面优于基线方法；2) 在Unitree G1双足机器人上实现零样本部署，成功在未见室内外环境中导航；3) 自然涌现出等待开门、绕开人群、避开玻璃墙等行为；4) 将发布数据集和训练模型。

Conclusion: EgoNav证明了仅从少量人类行走数据学习、无需机器人数据或微调，就能让双足机器人在多样未见环境中实现鲁棒导航。系统自然涌现出复杂导航行为，展示了从人类数据中学习导航先验的有效性。

Abstract: We present EgoNav, a system that enables a humanoid robot to traverse diverse, unseen environments by learning entirely from 5 hours of human walking data, with no robot data or finetuning. A diffusion model predicts distributions of plausible future trajectories conditioned on past trajectory, a 360 deg visual memory fusing color, depth, and semantics, and video features from a frozen DINOv3 backbone that capture appearance cues invisible to depth sensors. A hybrid sampling scheme achieves real-time inference in 10 denoising steps, and a receding-horizon controller selects paths from the predicted distribution. We validate EgoNav through offline evaluations, where it outperforms baselines in collision avoidance and multi-modal coverage, and through zero-shot deployment on a Unitree G1 humanoid across unseen indoor and outdoor environments. Behaviors such as waiting for doors to open, navigating around crowds, and avoiding glass walls emerge naturally from the learned prior. We will release the dataset and trained models. Our website: https://egonav.weizhuowang.com

</details>


### [177] [Certificate-Driven Closed-Loop Multi-Agent Path Finding with Inheritable Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00428)
*Jiarui Li,Runyu Zhang,Gioele Zardini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CDCBS算法，通过证书轨迹和舰队预算机制改进ACCBS，在密集场景下获得更一致的解质量，同时通过预算限制分解减少有效组大小


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有闭环MAPF算法（如ACCBS）在有限计算预算下可能因短视的有限视野规划而终止于短活动前缀，特别是在密集实例中，难以保持全局保证和利用组合结构

Method: 引入证书轨迹及其关联的舰队预算作为过滤闭环更新的通用机制。证书提供无冲突回退计划和剩余成本的单调上界；仅接受证书改进的更新可确保完备性。预算信息诱导出预算限制分解，实现跨时间步的全局可继承分解。将该框架实例化于ACCBS得到CDCBS

Result: 在基准地图上的实验表明，CDCBS比ACCBS获得更一致的解质量，特别是在密集设置中，同时提出的分解方法减少了有效组大小

Conclusion: 证书轨迹和舰队预算机制为闭环MAPF算法提供了一种通用框架，通过证书改进更新确保完备性，通过预算限制分解实现全局可继承分解，CDCBS实例化验证了该框架的有效性

Abstract: Multi-agent coordination in automated warehouses and logistics is commonly modeled as the Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) problem. Closed-loop MAPF algorithms improve scalability by planning only the next movement and replanning online, but this finite-horizon viewpoint can be shortsighted and makes it difficult to preserve global guarantees and exploit compositional structure. This issue is especially visible in Anytime Closed-Loop Conflict-Based Search (ACCBS), which applies Conflict-Based Search (CBS) over dynamically extended finite horizons but, under finite computational budgets, may terminate with short active prefixes in dense instances.
  We introduce certificate trajectories and their associated fleet budget as a general mechanism for filtering closed-loop updates. A certificate provides a conflict-free fallback plan and a monotone upper bound on the remaining cost; accepting only certificate-improving updates yields completeness. The same budget information induces a budget-limited factorization that enables global, inheritable decomposition across timesteps. Instantiating the framework on ACCBS yields Certificate-Driven Conflict-Based Search (CDCBS). Experiments on benchmark maps show that CDCBS achieves more consistent solution quality than ACCBS, particularly in dense settings, while the proposed factorization reduces effective group size.

</details>


### [178] [Reachability-Aware Time Scaling for Path Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00439)
*Hossein Gholampour,Logan E. Beaver*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于平面双积分系统的路径跟踪方法，通过离线速度缩放和在线QP跟踪相结合，确保在加速度限制下安全跟踪包含急转弯的高曲率路径


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于采样的离线规划器生成的路径可能包含急转弯和高曲率区域，即使在路径几何上无碰撞，加速度限制下的单步可达性也变得至关重要。需要确保在系统速度和加速度约束下能够安全跟踪这些路径

Method: 1. 离线阶段：在样条拟合的路径上评估单步加速度裕度，更新标量速度缩放曲线，使所需加速度保持在可用界限内
2. 在线阶段：采用纯追踪风格、可达性引导的二次规划跟踪器，使用相同的向前看跟踪结构跟踪缩放后的参考路径

Result: 该方法能够有效处理包含急转弯和高曲率区域的路径，通过速度缩放确保在加速度限制下的单步可达性，实现安全跟踪

Conclusion: 提出的离线速度缩放与在线QP跟踪相结合的方法，能够在速度和加速度约束下安全跟踪离线规划器生成的高曲率路径，解决了采样规划路径中急转弯带来的跟踪挑战

Abstract: This paper studies tracking of collision-free waypoint paths produced by an offline planner for a planar double-integrator system with bounded speed and acceleration. Because sampling-based planners must route around obstacles, the resulting waypoint paths can contain sharp turns and high-curvature regions, so one-step reachability under acceleration limits becomes critical even when the path geometry is collision-free. We build on a pure-pursuit-style, reachability-guided quadratic-program (QP) tracker with a one-step acceleration margin. Offline, we evaluate this margin along a spline fitted to the waypoint path and update a scalar speed-scaling profile so that the required one-step acceleration remains below the available bound. Online, the same look-ahead tracking structure is used to track the scaled reference.

</details>


### [179] [Multi-Camera View Scaling for Data-Efficient Robot Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00557)
*Yichen Xie,Yixiao Wang,Shuqi Zhao,Cheng-En Wu,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Jianwen Xie,Hao-Shu Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过多视角相机采集伪演示数据，无需额外人工标注即可提升模仿学习的泛化能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习策略的泛化能力受限于专家演示的多样性，而收集多样化环境的演示成本高且困难。需要一种无需额外人工努力就能利用场景多样性的实用方法。

Method: 提出一个实用框架：在演示采集时扩展相机视角，使用多个同步相机视角从每个专家轨迹生成伪演示，丰富训练分布并提升视觉表示中的视角不变性。分析不同动作空间与视角扩展的交互，展示相机空间表示如何进一步增强多样性。引入多视角动作聚合方法，使单视角策略在部署时也能受益于多个相机。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界操作任务中的大量实验表明，相比单视角基线，该方法在数据效率和泛化能力方面有显著提升。

Conclusion: 扩展相机视角为模仿学习提供了一个实用且可扩展的解决方案，只需最少的额外硬件设置，并能与现有模仿学习算法无缝集成。

Abstract: The generalization ability of imitation learning policies for robotic manipulation is fundamentally constrained by the diversity of expert demonstrations, while collecting demonstrations across varied environments is costly and difficult in practice. In this paper, we propose a practical framework that exploits inherent scene diversity without additional human effort by scaling camera views during demonstration collection. Instead of acquiring more trajectories, multiple synchronized camera perspectives are used to generate pseudo-demonstrations from each expert trajectory, which enriches the training distribution and improves viewpoint invariance in visual representations. We analyze how different action spaces interact with view scaling and show that camera-space representations further enhance diversity. In addition, we introduce a multiview action aggregation method that allows single-view policies to benefit from multiple cameras during deployment. Extensive experiments in simulation and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate significant gains in data efficiency and generalization compared to single-view baselines. Our results suggest that scaling camera views provides a practical and scalable solution for imitation learning, which requires minimal additional hardware setup and integrates seamlessly with existing imitation learning algorithms. The website of our project is https://yichen928.github.io/robot_multiview.

</details>


### [180] [A Physical Imitation Learning Pipeline for Energy-Efficient Quadruped Locomotion Assisted by Parallel Elastic Joint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00611)
*Huyue Ma,Yurui Jin,Helmut Hauser,Rui Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PIL（物理模仿学习）将强化学习控制策略蒸馏到可物理实现的被动并联弹性关节中，让机器人身体模仿部分控制行为，从而降低能耗


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物通过脑体协同进化，利用内在身体动力学实现节能运动，而机器人通常采用集中控制器抑制而非利用身体动力学。需要开发能利用身体被动特性的控制方法

Method: 物理模仿学习（PIL）：将强化学习控制策略蒸馏到被动并联弹性关节（PEJs）中，让身体模仿部分控制行为，剩余部分由电机控制恢复性能

Result: 在模拟四足机器人中，PIL可将高达87%的机械功率卸载到PEJs（平坦地形），粗糙地形为18%，显著降低能耗

Conclusion: PIL实现了脑体协同设计而不扩展搜索空间，为关节型机器人形态提供了计算高效的任务特定物理智能实现途径

Abstract: Due to brain-body co-evolution, animals' intrinsic body dynamics play a crucial role in energy-efficient locomotion, which shares control effort between active muscles and passive body dynamics -- a principle known as Embodied Physical Intelligence. In contrast, robot bodies are often designed with one centralised controller that typically suppress the intrinsic body dynamics instead of exploiting it. We introduce Physical Imitation Learning (PIL), which distils a Reinforcement Learning (RL) control policy into physically implementable body responses that can be directly offloaded to passive Parallel Elastic Joints (PEJs), enabling therefore the body to imitate part of the controlled behaviour. Meanwhile, the residual policy commands the motors to recover the RL policy's performance. The results is an overall reduced energy consumption thanks to outsourcing parts of the control policy to the PEJs. Here we show in simulated quadrupeds, that our PIL approach can offloads up to 87% of mechanical power to PEJs on flat terrain and 18% on rough terrain. Because the body design is distilled from -- rather than jointly optimised with -- the control policy, PIL realises brain-body co-design without expanding the search space with body design parameters, providing a computationally efficient route to task-specific Embodied Physical Intelligence applicable to a wide range of joint-based robot morphologies.

</details>


### [181] [StretchBot: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Adaptive Guidance with Assistive Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00628)
*Luca Vogelgesang,Ahmed Mehdi Soltani,Mohammadhossein Khojasteh,Xinrui Zu,Stefano De Giorgis,Madalina Croitoru,Filip Ilievski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StretchBot：一种结合多模态感知与知识图谱增强大语言模型推理的混合神经符号机器人教练，用于自适应辅助拉伸指导


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有辅助机器人系统大多采用脚本化设计，无法适应用户状态、环境背景和交互动态的变化，限制了其在家庭和医疗场景中支持身体健康的潜力

Method: 开发StretchBot系统，结合多模态感知与基于知识图谱的大语言模型推理，支持短时拉伸会话中的上下文感知调整，同时保持结构化常规

Result: 探索性试点比较（3名参与者）显示：自适应条件在感知适应性和上下文相关性方面表现更好，而脚本化指导在流畅性和可预测性方面仍具竞争力

Conclusion: 结构化可操作知识有助于在具身辅助交互中基于语言模型进行自适应，但需要更大规模、纵向研究来评估鲁棒性、泛化能力和长期用户体验

Abstract: Assistive robots have growing potential to support physical wellbeing in home and healthcare settings, for example, by guiding users through stretching or rehabilitation routines. However, existing systems remain largely scripted, which limits their ability to adapt to user state, environmental context, and interaction dynamics. In this work, we present StretchBot, a hybrid neuro-symbolic robotic coach for adaptive assistive guidance. The system combines multimodal perception with knowledge-graph-grounded large language model reasoning to support context-aware adjustments during short stretching sessions while maintaining a structured routine. To complement the system description, we report an exploratory pilot comparison between scripted and adaptive guidance with three participants. The pilot findings suggest that the adaptive condition improved perceived adaptability and contextual relevance, while scripted guidance remained competitive in smoothness and predictability. These results provide preliminary evidence that structured actionable knowledge can help ground language-model-based adaptation in embodied assistive interaction, while also highlighting the need for larger, longitudinal studies to evaluate robustness, generalizability, and long-term user experience.

</details>


### [182] [LiPS: Lightweight Panoptic Segmentation for Resource-Constrained Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00634)
*Calvin Galagain,Martyna Poreba,François Goulette,Cyrill Stachniss*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LiPS：一种轻量级全景分割方法，在保持查询式解码的同时简化特征提取与融合，显著降低计算需求，适合资源受限的移动机器人平台


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的全景分割模型复杂度高，不适合部署在移动机器人等资源受限平台上，需要一种高效计算的全景分割方法

Method: 提出LiPS方法，保留基于查询的解码机制，同时引入简化的特征提取和融合路径，实现轻量级设计

Result: 在标准基准测试中，LiPS达到与更重基线相当的精度，同时提供高达4.5倍的吞吐量（帧/秒），计算需求减少近6.8倍

Conclusion: LiPS的高效性使其成为现代全景分割模型与现实世界机器人应用之间的重要桥梁

Abstract: Panoptic segmentation is a key enabler for robotic perception, as it unifies semantic understanding with object-level reasoning. However, the increasing complexity of state-of-the-art models makes them unsuitable for deployment on resource-constrained platforms such as mobile robots. We propose a novel approach called LiPS that addresses the challenge of efficient-to-compute panoptic segmentation with a lightweight design that retains query-based decoding while introducing a streamlined feature extraction and fusion pathway. It aims at providing a strong panoptic segmentation performance while substantially lowering the computational demands. Evaluations on standard benchmarks demonstrate that LiPS attains accuracy comparable to much heavier baselines, while providing up to 4.5 higher throughput, measured in frames per second, and requiring nearly 6.8 times fewer computations. This efficiency makes LiPS a highly relevant bridge between modern panoptic models and real-world robotic applications.

</details>


### [183] [SoftHand Model-W: A 3D-Printed, Anthropomorphic, Underactuated Robot Hand with Integrated Wrist and Carpal Tunnel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00738)
*Dhillon B. Merritt,Christopher J. Ford,Haoran Li,Malia Smith,Zhixing Chen,Efi Psomopoulou,Nathan F. Lepora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SoftHand Model-W：一款基于Pisa/IIT SoftHand的3D打印欠驱动拟人机器人手，集成拮抗肌腱机制和2自由度肌腱驱动腕部，通过腕部主动运动减少手臂补偿动作，提高操作效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软体机器人手通常缺乏腕部自由度，导致需要手臂进行大量补偿性运动来完成精细操作任务，限制了操作效率和灵活性。需要开发具有腕部主动运动能力的紧凑型机器人手，以实现更自然、高效的人机交互操作。

Method: 基于Pisa/IIT SoftHand设计3D打印的欠驱动拟人机器人手，集成拮抗肌腱机制和2自由度肌腱驱动腕部（屈伸和桡尺偏）。采用腕管启发的肌腱布线，将电机远程放置在手臂中，减少远端惯性。将SoftHand-W安装在6轴机器人手臂上进行立方体堆叠和平面圆盘旋转测试，比较有无腕部驱动时的任务性能。

Result: 实验结果显示，添加腕部驱动显著减少了手臂的补偿性和重新配置运动，缩短了任务完成时间。腕部使原本不可能实现的拾取放置操作成为可能。具体表现为：任务时间减少、手臂关节行程减少、配置变化减少。

Conclusion: SoftHand Model-W证明了近端自由度对于实现真实世界机器人应用中多功能、类人操作的关键作用。紧凑的设计使其适用于研究和辅助场景，腕部主动运动能力显著提高了操作效率和灵活性。

Abstract: This paper presents the SoftHand Model-W: a 3D-printed, underactuated, anthropomorphic robot hand based on the Pisa/IIT SoftHand, with an integrated antagonistic tendon mechanism and 2 degree-of-freedom tendon-driven wrist. These four degrees-of-acuation provide active flexion and extension to the five fingers, and active flexion/extension and radial/ulnar deviation of the palm through the wrist, while preserving the synergistic and self-adaptive features of such SoftHands. A carpal tunnel-inspired tendon routing allows remote motor placement in the forearm, reducing distal inertia and maintaining a compact form factor. The SoftHand-W is mounted on a 6-axis robot arm and tested with two reorientation tasks requiring coordination between the hand and arm's pose: cube stacking and in-plane disc rotation. Results comparing task time, arm joint travel, and configuration changes with and without wrist actuation show that adding the wrist reduces compensatory and reconfiguration movements of the arm for a quicker task-completion time. Moreover, the wrist enables pick-and-place operations that would be impossible otherwise. Overall, the SoftHand Model-W demonstrates how proximal degrees of freedom are key to achieving versatile, human-like manipulation in real world robotic applications, with a compact design enabling deployment in research and assistive settings.

</details>


### [184] [How to Train your Tactile Model: Tactile Perception with Multi-fingered Robot Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00744)
*Christopher J. Ford,Kaichen Shi,Laura Butcher,Nathan F. Lepora,Efi Psomopoulou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacViT：基于视觉Transformer的新型触觉感知模型，旨在解决CNN在新传感器部署中需要大量传感器特定数据和重新训练的问题，通过全局自注意力机制实现对新传感器的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于CNN的触觉感知方法存在严重局限性：1）需要大量传感器特定数据集；2）对新传感器需要重新训练（由于镜头特性、照明条件和传感器磨损差异）；3）限制了多指机器人手中视觉触觉传感器的快速部署和可扩展性。

Method: 提出TacViT模型，基于视觉Transformer架构，利用全局自注意力机制从触觉图像中提取鲁棒特征。该设计使模型能够学习更通用的触觉表示，从而在未见过的传感器上也能准确推断接触属性。

Result: 在五指机器人手的传感器上评估TacViT，结果显示其泛化性能显著优于CNN方法。TacViT能够在新传感器上保持准确接触属性推断，大幅减少数据收集和重新训练需求。

Conclusion: TacViT通过Transformer架构的全局自注意力机制实现了对新触觉传感器的有效泛化，显著减少了部署新传感器所需的数据和训练时间，使触觉感知在实际机器人应用中更具可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: Rapid deployment of new tactile sensors is essential for scalable robotic manipulation, especially in multi-fingered hands equipped with vision-based tactile sensors. However, current methods for inferring contact properties rely heavily on convolutional neural networks (CNNs), which, while effective on known sensors, require large, sensor-specific datasets. Furthermore, they require retraining for each new sensor due to differences in lens properties, illumination, and sensor wear. Here we introduce TacViT, a novel tactile perception model based on Vision Transformers, designed to generalize on new sensor data. TacViT leverages global self-attention mechanisms to extract robust features from tactile images, enabling accurate contact property inference even on previously unseen sensors. This capability significantly reduces the need for data collection and retraining, accelerating the deployment of new sensors. We evaluate TacViT on sensors for a five-fingered robot hand and demonstrate its superior generalization performance compared to CNNs. Our results highlight TacViTs potential to make tactile sensing more scalable and practical for real-world robotic applications.

</details>


### [185] [A wearable haptic device for edge and surface simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00752)
*Rui Chen,Xianlong Mai,Alireza Sanaei,Domenico Chiaradia,Antonio Frisoli,Daniele Leonardis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级指尖触觉设备，通过双电机机制区分表面和边缘接触反馈，增强VR中的物体操作沉浸感


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统指尖触觉设备无法渲染某些对沉浸式和精确交互相关的触觉特征（如边缘检测），限制了VR中物体操作的逼真度

Method: 开发紧凑轻量（24.3克）的指尖触觉设备，采用新型双电机机制提供可区分的表面和边缘接触反馈；使用6×6柔性传感器阵列进行压力分布表征；进行初步用户研究评估设备性能

Result: 压力分布表征显示两种刺激模式具有明显不同的接触模式；5名参与者的用户研究在四种条件（边缘/表面接触×轻/重压力）下达到93%的平均分类准确率，平均响应时间2.79秒

Conclusion: 该设备能有效传递边缘和表面触觉线索，有望提高VR环境中物体操作的逼真度

Abstract: Object manipulation is fundamental to virtual reality (VR) applications, yet conventional fingertip haptic devices fail to render certain tactile features relevant for immersive and precise interactions, as i.e. detection of edges. This paper presents a compact, lightweight fingertip haptic device (24.3 g) that delivers distinguishable surface and edge contact feedback through a novel dual-motor mechanism. Pressure distribution characterization using a 6 x 6 flexible sensor array demonstrates distinct contact patterns between the two stimulation modes. A preliminary user study with five participants achieved 93% average classification accuracy across four conditions (edge/surface contact with light/heavy pressure), with mean response times of 2.79 seconds. The results indicate that the proposed device can effectively convey edge and surface tactile cues, potentially enhancing object manipulation fidelity in VR environments.

</details>


### [186] [A Dual-Action Fabric-Based Soft Robotic Glove for Ergonomic Hand Rehabilitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00768)
*Rui Chen,Firman Isma Serdana,Domenico Chiaradia,Xianlong Mai,Elena Losanno,Gabriele Righi,Claudia De Santis,Federica Serra,Vincent Mendez,Cristian Camardella,Daniele Leonardis,Giulio Del Popolo,Silvestro Micera,Antonio Frisoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一种基于织物的双作用软体机器人手套，具有个性化定制的执行器，支持手指屈伸和拇指外展，通过系统表征和初步研究验证了其辅助效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经系统疾病导致的手部功能障碍严重影响日常生活独立性，现有软体机器人手套在定制化、人体工程学适配和屈伸驱动方面存在限制，制约了其临床应用价值。

Method: 采用计算机数控热封技术制造对称腔室执行器，设计具有五个独立控制的双作用执行器支持手指屈伸，以及专门的拇指外展执行器。执行器充气时形成凹形外表面以最大化手指接触面积。

Result: 执行器产生足够的关节力矩和指尖力以满足日常生活任务需求，完整手套系统对常见家居物品产生足够的抓握力。健康受试者研究显示手套辅助显著降低前臂肌肉活动；脊髓损伤患者初步研究表明手套促进更自然的抓握模式并减少对肌腱固定抓握的依赖，但任务完成时间增加。

Conclusion: 这种可定制、符合人体工程学的设计代表了向个性化手部康复和辅助机器人技术迈出的实用一步，尽管当前驱动界面仍需优化以减少任务完成时间。

Abstract: Hand impairment following neurological disorders substantially limits independence in activities of daily living, motivating the development of effective assistive and rehabilitation strategies. Soft robotic gloves have attracted growing interest in this context, yet persistent challenges in customization, ergonomic fit, and flexion-extension actuation constrain their clinical utility. Here, we present a dual-action fabric-based soft robotic glove incorporating customized actuators aligned with individual finger joints. The glove comprises five independently controlled dual-action actuators supporting finger flexion and extension, together with a dedicated thumb abduction actuator. Leveraging computer numerical control heat sealing technology, we fabricated symmetrical-chamber actuators that adopt a concave outer surface upon inflation, thereby maximizing finger contact area and improving comfort. Systematic characterization confirmed that the actuators generate sufficient joint moment and fingertip force for ADL-relevant tasks, and that the complete glove system produces adequate grasping force for common household objects. A preliminary study with ten healthy subjects demonstrated that active glove assistance significantly reduces forearm muscle activity during object manipulation. A pilot feasibility study with three individuals with cervical spinal cord injury across seven functional tasks indicated that glove assistance promotes more natural grasp patterns and reduces reliance on tenodesis grasp, although at the cost of increased task completion time attributable to the current actuation interface. This customizable, ergonomic design represents a practical step toward personalized hand rehabilitation and assistive robotics.

</details>


### [187] [Compact Keyframe-Optimized Multi-Agent Gaussian Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00804)
*Monica M. Q. Li,Pierre-Yves Lajoie,Jialiang Liu,Giovanni Beltrame*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出改进的多智能体RGB-D高斯泼溅SLAM框架，通过压缩冗余高斯和两种闭环检测模式，在保持地图保真度的同时减少85-95%的通信负载


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体3D建图中，密集表示（特别是高斯泼溅方法生成的地图）在有限带宽下实时交换困难，现有集中式SLAM系统在合并本地地图时面临通信瓶颈

Method: 1) 在SLAM系统中加入压缩步骤去除冗余3D高斯而不降低渲染质量；2) 提出无需初始猜测的集中式闭环检测，包含两种模式：纯渲染深度模式（仅需3D高斯）和相机深度模式（包含轻量深度图像以提高配准精度和进一步高斯修剪）

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的评估显示，与最先进方法相比，两种模式下传输数据减少85-95%，使3D高斯多智能体SLAM更接近实际部署

Conclusion: 该方法通过有效压缩和优化的闭环检测，显著降低了多智能体高斯泼溅SLAM的通信负载，同时保持地图质量，推动了该技术在实际场景中的应用

Abstract: Efficient multi-agent 3D mapping is essential for robotic teams operating in unknown environments, but dense representations hinder real-time exchange over constrained communication links. In multi-agent Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM), systems typically rely on a centralized server to merge and optimize the local maps produced by individual agents. However, sharing these large map representations, particularly those generated by recent methods such as Gaussian Splatting, becomes a bottleneck in real-world scenarios with limited bandwidth. We present an improved multi-agent RGB-D Gaussian Splatting SLAM framework that reduces communication load while preserving map fidelity. First, we incorporate a compaction step into our SLAM system to remove redundant 3D Gaussians, without degrading the rendering quality. Second, our approach performs centralized loop closure computation without initial guess, operating in two modes: a pure rendered-depth mode that requires no data beyond the 3D Gaussians, and a camera-depth mode that includes lightweight depth images for improved registration accuracy and additional Gaussian pruning. Evaluation on both synthetic and real-world datasets shows up to 85-95\% reduction in transmitted data compared to state-of-the-art approaches in both modes, bringing 3D Gaussian multi-agent SLAM closer to practical deployment in real-world scenarios. Code: https://github.com/lemonci/coko-slam

</details>


### [188] [PanoAir: A Panoramic Visual-Inertial SLAM with Cross-Time Real-World UAV Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00852)
*Yiyang Wu,Xiaohu Zhang,Yanjin Du,Tongsu Zhang,Chujun Li,Siyang Chen,Guoyi Zhang,Xiangpeng Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个用于无人机应用的全景视觉-惯性SLAM框架，并构建了首个真实世界的全景视觉-惯性数据集，通过全景特征提取和闭环检测提高了姿态估计的准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-惯性SLAM方法主要依赖有限视场传感器，在复杂无人机场景中容易产生漂移甚至失效。全景相机提供全向感知能力但相关研究和数据集仍不足，需要开发适用于无人机的全景VI-SLAM解决方案。
